The new native performance entry point describes the actual complete Engine plan: explicit target or saved automatic ceiling, draft activation/depth/placement, admitted lookahead and correction identity, prefix retention, live memory management and GPU keep-alive policy. Input token IDs and complete reply reservations are frozen. Only the original and admitted grouped minmax artifact are available. The public supported-pack registry remains unchanged.

Fixed-work generation uses a package-only Engine entry point that ignores EOS inside a bounded diagnostic while retaining the ordinary admission, context, allocation, cancellation, text and cache path. This artificial workload cannot count as completed-task quality. Separate natural requests retain normal termination. Receipts preserve native committed-token intervals, decoded-text callbacks, startup time, requested/applied plan snapshots, prefix reuse, actual memory, system paging and thermal/power observations. Text callback counts never stand in for token counts. The native owner enforces its physical ceiling, three-GB live headroom, normal OS pressure and deadlines; a governor retains the requested automatic or fixed live mode. Complete paired execution, eligibility and final scenario/sample-count freeze are still required.

The conservative generation metric excludes the first token from its numerator, because its logits came from prefill, and retains the entire post-prefill decode timer, including terminal work. Active-emission rate, visible-text rate, full latency and tail stalls remain separate observations. A one-token response has latency but no decode-speed estimate. Review removed an unjustified closeness test between clocks with different start boundaries; the outer decoded-text callback must follow the inner observation, with both origins retained.

Eight Python groups pass on both runtimes, including nonfinite/count failures, speculative bursts, post-emission work, one-token responses, exact rank witnesses, exhaustive low/high rank enumeration and simultaneous scenario coverage. Thirty-two static entry-point checks pass with the suite registered. The native protocol refusal/catalogue checks and physical measurement entry point have not yet been compiled or run. No model was launched and no performance result was produced during preparation.

The read-only metric helper uses a conservative one-sided binomial order-statistic bound for the median, with the complete scenario family accounted for by Bonferroni. [NIST's median confidence reference](https://itl.nist.gov/div898/software/dataplot/refman1/auxillar/mediancl.htm) describes the binomial rank basis. The implementation uses exact rational tail probabilities without interpolation. Its coverage assumes independent, identically distributed run-level samples from the declared operating condition; correlated token intervals are not trials. An insufficient number of repetitions cannot yield a finite lower bound. The helper never grants model qualification. A later prospective study must freeze eligibility, all scenarios and repetitions before final observations.

Full engine CI at 54811942cd622d982291c0693b593039a2a7eaf0 passes weights-free, coverage and public-library jobs. Full engine and Mac checks/Xcode CI at 454209c6d377ebeefd2290e2866a48fbd3d72d88 also pass. These close the continuation and explicit startup-observation source checks, not the unfinished quality study or future optimization. The first read-only CI retrieval failed to connect and is recorded; subsequent complete receipts and logs are preserved below.
